Evidence: `A08_V2_RETEST_PATH_AND_RESUME_HANDOFF_2026-11-26.md` (P04), which read the two files in full, the V2 wiring and the resumption at the interface; Python date-time, dataclass and threading behaviour is current external context read on 26-Nov-2026, with plain-expression checks on the work environment's interpreter (P04-S005, P04-S006).

**The files, the switch and the three "retest" mechanisms.**

- FACT: `sr_detector/retest_confirm.py` 137 lines; `screening/retest_monitor.py` 441 lines (P04-S011). `retest_confirm.py` imports only `annotations`, `dataclass` and `List`, and defines the states `WAIT_BREAKOUT`, `WAIT_RETEST`, `WAIT_CONFIRM`, `CONFIRMED`, `REJECT`; `retest_monitor.py` imports only the standard library and `RetestParams`, `evaluate` (P04-S012, P04-S013).
- HISTORICAL: "The WAIT_FOR_RETEST confirmation state machine — PURE and unit-testable. No candle patterns / indicator names; only "broke + touched + strong close""; "The WAIT_FOR_RETEST monitor. Holds candidates DIVERTED by the pre-placement check (a LONG entry inside a HIGH resistance zone) and, on a daemon poll thread, fetches fresh 1m candles per parked symbol" (P04-S014, P04-S015).
- FACT: `_v2_on = getattr(_sr_cfg, "wait_for_retest_enabled", False)` (`main.py` L3423); the zone cache, structure fetcher and warmer are built under `_v2_on or _struct_exit_on`; the one-minute fetcher, `RetestParams`, `RetestMonitor` and `RetestDiverter` are built, bound and started only under `if _v2_on and zone_warmer is not None and _v2_zone_cache is not None:` (L3769 to L3802); the committed value is `false` (`config/system_config.yaml` L529; loader default `False`) (P04-S016). INTERPRETATION: at the committed values none of the V2 objects exists, the processor's `_retest_diverter` stays `None` and the divert test at L1042 is false; whether the switch was ever true is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (P04-S016; P04-U01).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: three distinct "retest" mechanisms exist in the code: (a) the V1 observer's post-placement proposal, written to `sr_detector_results` only (section 5); (b) the V2 path studied here, which parks a screened signal before sizing and places a MARKET entry on confirmation; (c) A18's PB-01 next-morning entry stage (`v3_chain/pb01_entry.py`), its own module, thread and table, which calls itself "the direct analog of `screening/retest_monitor.py::RetestMonitor`", imports nothing from the two V2 files and binds its `on_confirm` to its own recorder (`main.py` L3745) (P04-S017).

**`retest_confirm`: parameters, states and `evaluate`.**

- FACT: `_is_long` is `str(direction).strip().upper() in ("LONG", "BUY")` (P04-S018). `RetestParams` holds `timeout_sec`, `max_away_pct` (the wrong-way reject), `confirm_strong_close_frac` and `breakout_margin_pct`; `RetestResult` holds `state` and `reason` with the properties `confirmed`, `rejected` and `terminal` (P04-S019, P04-S020). `evaluate` can return exactly eight results: `REJECT` "timeout", `REJECT` "break_down" (long) or "reclaim_up" (short), `CONFIRMED` "reclaim_strong_close" (long) or "rejection_strong_close" (short), and the three in-progress states (P04-S021, P04-S022).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: the first test is `elapsed_sec > timeout_sec`, before any candle, so the timeout overrides every candle; the function has no clock of its own and runs on the caller's `elapsed_sec` (P04-S023). For a long, a break is a one-minute close strictly above `band_high × (1 + margin/100)` (at the committed `breakout_margin_pct` 0.0, any close above the top), the wrong-way reject a close strictly below `band_low × (1 − max_away_pct/100)`, the touch a candle whose range re-enters the band (its low at or below the band's top and its high at or above the band's bottom), and the confirmation a further break with a strong close; the short side mirrors (P04-S024, P04-S025). The fold checks the reject on every candle and makes at most one transition per candle, so a confirmation needs at least three candles (P04-S026, P04-S027).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: `_strong_close` requires, for a long, `(close − low) / (high − low) >= frac`; with the committed 0.6 the close must be in the top 40 % of the range; a bar of zero range is strong when its close is at or above its high (long) or at or below its low (short), so a flat bar is strong for both sides, whatever the fraction (P04-S029, P04-S030). The fold reads no `ts` and does not sort or de-duplicate, so order and duplicates can change its result (A05 CM-05) (P04-S028, P04-S155). NaN values never break or fail; bands and parameters outside the committed values are not checked (P04-S031, P04-S032). INTERPRETATION: `evaluate` is a pure function of its arguments, as its docstring's "pure, idempotent and restart-safe" says (P04-S033).

**`RetestDiverter`: the divert decision.**

- FACT: the one construction (`main.py` L3789 to L3792) passes the zone cache, the monitor, the store, `_sr_cfg`, the clock `time_authority.now_ist` and the mode; the diverter reads `wait_for_retest_enabled`, `near_zone_buffer_pct`, `require_confidence` and `sl_buffer_pct` from the configuration (P04-S034, P04-S035).
- FACT (call site; A06's file, interface only): in `_process_one`, after the screener's `PASSED`, the price derivation and the side mapping and before the FIX-067 re-anchor, sizing, the V3 chain observation and the allocator routing, the processor calls `maybe_divert(...)` with eleven keyword arguments (L1042 to L1049); on `True` it logs "diverted to WAIT_FOR_RETEST ... — no capital reserved" and returns; this is the only call (P04-S036, P04-S117). INTERPRETATION (A07 EG-01): every signal the screener passes on that route can be diverted, whatever its strategy (no `pullback_wait_enabled`, intent or strategy test); the gate resumption, the allocator's admission and the retest resumption never pass L1042 (P04-S037, P04-S141).
- FACT: `maybe_divert` returns `False` when disabled, for a side other than `BUY` or `SELL`, on a cache miss or expired entry, or when no zone matches; it drops the signal as `REJECTED_RETEST_DUP` ("symbol already parked in WAIT_FOR_RETEST") when the symbol is already parked; otherwise it computes the structure stop, builds the `ParkedCandidate`, calls `register`, writes `RETEST_WAITING` and writes the audit row, and returns `True`; an exception before the commit returns `False`, after it `True` (P04-S038, P04-S044). The match takes, among the side's zones of the required confidence (committed `HIGH`) whose band widened by `near_zone_buffer_pct` (committed 0.3) contains the entry, the one with the nearest centre (P04-S039).
- INTERPRETATION: the diverter neither fetches nor asks for a warm, so whether a symbol's first signal in a process can be diverted depends on whether its warm finished first, a race whose outcomes are UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED; a hit can rest on zones up to 1800 s old built from candles older still (P04-S040). The duplicate test runs only after a zone match and ignores the direction, so a later signal of a parked symbol that matches no zone goes to normal placement while the parked candidate can still resume; `has_symbol` and `register` are separate critical sections, and the comment L329 to L331 attributes their serialization to A06's upstream `_claim_in_flight` (HISTORICAL) (P04-S041).
- FACT: the structure stop is `band_low × (1 − sl_buffer_pct/100)` for a long and `band_high × (1 + sl_buffer_pct/100)` for a short (committed 0.2); it is used only for the audit row's `proposed_retest_sl`, is stored neither in the `ParkedCandidate` nor in `retest_state`, and the resumption computes its own (P04-S042). The `ParkedCandidate` carries the zone band, the derived entry and stop, the strategy, intent, tier, state `WAIT_BREAKOUT`, the trigger price, the sizing inputs and a naive `added_at` (the clock made naive by `replace(tzinfo=None)`, L368) (P04-S043). The audit row writes one `sr_detector_results` row with `detector_version` `snr-v2`, best effort (P04-S045). INTERPRETATION: a successful divert leaves `RETEST_WAITING`, one `retest_state` row and one audit row (P04-S046). FACT: the processor writes the same INFO line for a duplicate drop as for a parking (P04-S183).

**`ParkedCandidate` and `retest_state` (A03 H-11, P-02, Q-09, H-25, H-47, Q-46).**

- FACT: `ParkedCandidate` is a frozen dataclass of fourteen fields; `_to_row` writes sixteen keys, the times `added_at` and `created_at` as naive texts and `timeout_at` as `added_at + timeout_sec`; `_from_row` restores them (P04-S048 to P04-S052). INTERPRETATION: the times in `retest_state` are naive IST texts (A03 H-11) (P04-S051).
- FACT: the table is "TABLE 39: retest_state (SNR-V2 Phase A, schema v38)"; v38 also rebuilt `signals` to widen its status CHECK with `GLOB 'RETEST_*'`; the store's `insert_retest_state` is an `INSERT OR REPLACE` keyed by the signal ID's sub-select, and `release_retest_state` deletes the row and writes the signal's status in one transaction (A03's, interface) (P04-S053 to P04-S056). INTERPRETATION: every value A08 writes fits the CHECKs (A03 H-25, H-47), and the upsert keeps one row per signal (A03 Q-46); whether two concurrent inserts for one signal could both find no row is A03's to judge, and nothing in the table limits the rows of one symbol (P04-S057, P04-S058, P04-S159). FACT: the retest path writes six status values: `RETEST_WAITING` and `REJECTED_RETEST_DUP` through `update_signal_status`, and `RETEST_CONFIRMED`, `RETEST_REJECTED_TIMEOUT`, `RETEST_REJECTED_BREAK_DOWN` and `RETEST_REJECTED_RECLAIM_UP` through `release_retest_state`; no call passes a time stamp and none checks the stored status first (A03 P-02, Q-09; A06 EV-01) (P04-S056, P04-S133, P04-S144, P04-S146). FACT: A12's `scripts/eod_cleanup.py` deletes `retest_state` rows with pruned signals; A15's status report and the dashboard reader classify the `RETEST_*` statuses (P04-S059, P04-S060).

**`RetestMonitor`: lifecycle, poll loop, confirmation and rejection.**

- FACT: the one construction (`main.py` L3784 to L3788) passes the one-minute fetcher built at L3774 to L3777 (two days, TTL 0), the store, the parameters (`retest_timeout_sec` 1800, `retest_max_away_pct` 1.0, `confirm_strong_close_frac` 0.6, `breakout_margin_pct` 0.0; committed values), `on_confirm=signal_processor.continue_from_retest`, the poll interval (committed 20 s) and the clock `time_authority.now_ist`, and no interval, so the constructor default `"minute"` applies (P04-S061, P04-S062; P06-S043 to P06-S046, P06-S062). `start` returns without a log line when disabled or started, and otherwise starts the daemon thread "sr-retest-monitor"; the loop waits one interval, then runs `poll_once`, whose per-candidate errors are logged at ERROR "evaluate failed" (P04-S063 to P04-S065).
- FACT: `_evaluate` fetches the minute candles since `added_at` (filtered by comparing wall-clock digits, offsets removed), computes `elapsed = now_fn() − added_at`, calls `evaluate`, and then releases with `RETEST_CONFIRMED` and calls `on_confirm`, or releases with `RETEST_REJECTED_<REASON>`, or writes a changed in-progress state back with `update_retest_state` (P04-S066, P04-S069). INTERPRETATION (A05-17, A05-29, A05 HA-03, HA-04): under the start-of-minute reading of a label the divert's own minute is left out of the fold, under the end reading it is kept; the monitor applies no as-of truncation, so a still-forming minute, if the broker sent one, enters the fold and can cause an immediate, irreversible terminal result (P04-S067, P04-S068, P04-S142, P04-S156).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (the elapsed-time clock at the committed wiring): every `ParkedCandidate` the non-test code makes has a naive `added_at`, while the monitor's `now_fn` is the aware `time_authority.now_ist`; by the Python documentation and the plain-expression check, the subtraction at L171 raises `TypeError` ("can't subtract offset-naive and offset-aware datetimes") at every call of `_evaluate`, after the minute fetch and before `evaluate`; `poll_once` catches it and logs ERROR "evaluate failed"; on the code and wiring at the baseline, with the switch on, the monitor would therefore never confirm, reject or time out any parked candidate: each would stay in memory and in `retest_state`, its signal at `RETEST_WAITING`, its symbol blocking later in-zone signals as `REJECTED_RETEST_DUP`, and its minute candles fetched again at every poll (P04-S070). The tests give the monitor a naive clock and so do not show it (P04-S172). INTERPRETATION: under a clock of the same form as `added_at`, as in the tests, the elapsed time counts wall-clock seconds since the divert, through non-market time and across restarts (P04-S071). Whether the `TypeError` occurred needs the switch on and is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (P04-U03).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (A03 Q-11): `_release` removes the candidate from memory first and is not undone when the store call fails; on a confirmation the callback then runs all the same, so the row and the old status `RETEST_WAITING` stay while the signal goes to `continue_from_retest`; a later restart restores such a row (P04-S072, P04-S135).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (A06 EV-11): the callback runs synchronously on "sr-retest-monitor" inside `poll_once`, after the release and the INFO line, passing the snapshot's `ParkedCandidate` (whose `state` is still the last in-progress state); the return value is discarded and an exception is logged; while it works the remaining candidates of the poll wait; within one process the monitor cannot call `on_confirm` twice for one signal, a second call needing a restart after a failed release or a second divert of the same signal (P04-S073, P04-S138). FACT: `register` persists the candidate inside one `try` ("Never raises"); `has_symbol` and `parked_count` read the dictionary under the lock (P04-S074, P04-S075).
- INTERPRETATION: a poll whose fetch gives no candles writes the snapshot back to `WAIT_BREAKOUT`, so the stored state is not monotonic; the outcome is not affected, the state not being an input (P04-S076). Each poll makes one historical request per parked candidate, one after another, through the shared closure and the limiter's historical bucket (P04-S077). FACT: the monitor's log lines are listed in P04-S078.

**Restart, end of day and shutdown.**

- FACT: `rehydrate` reads every `retest_state` row, converts each and parks it in memory, logging a malformed row and going on; every exception is caught inside the method; it is called at `main.py` L3795, after the diverter is bound and the end-of-day square-off is given the monitor, and before the warmer's and the monitor's starts (P04-S079, P04-S080). INTERPRETATION: rehydration restores every row whatever its date, its signal's status, its symbol (two rows of one symbol are both restored) or its age; `timeout_at` is ignored (P04-S081 to P04-S083). FACT and INTERPRETATION: no line of the monitor or the diverter reads the date, the session or a calendar, so rows left by a process that ran no end-of-day fire are restored on a later day; the end-of-day comment intends that none "survive into the next session" and A18's PB-01 stage states an anti-rehydration rule that loads "ONLY today's rows" (HISTORICAL) (P04-S084).
- FACT: `clear_all` empties memory under the lock and then calls `clear_all_retest_state` in a `try`, returning the in-memory count; its one call site is A12's `orders/eod_squareoff.py` `_fire` (L349 to L356) (P04-S085, P04-S086, P04-S119). INTERPRETATION: the cleared signals keep `RETEST_WAITING` with no final status and stay outside the prune predicate; a poll whose snapshot predates the clear can still release a cleared candidate and hand it to the resumption (P04-S087). A restart-recovery fire during `eod.post_wire_init()` (`main.py` L3866) would clear candidates just restored (P04-S088).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: `stop` sets the stop event, joins for 2.0 s and clears the worker reference whether or not the join completed (called at `main.py` L1733); a poll in progress, a fetch or a callback inside `continue_from_retest`, is not interrupted (P04-S089, P04-S090). FACT and INTERPRETATION (A01 C37): the V2 block's `except` sets only `retest_monitor = None`; if a thread start fails, the processor keeps the bound, enabled diverter and the square-off keeps the monitor while `_shutdown` receives `None`, so signals can be parked in a monitor that never polls (P04-S091, P04-S152). FACT and INTERPRETATION (A01 C13): the monitor's thread starts at L3797, before `eod.post_wire_init()` (L3866), the startup reconcile (L3880, which by its comment "MUST precede signal_processor.start() and the webhook server start", so that "NO new signal can be sized/placed while a phantom still holds capital") and the processor's start (L3974); by the code's structure its first poll, one interval after start, could hand a rehydrated candidate to `continue_from_retest` before those steps; at the committed wiring the evaluation raises first (P04-S092, P04-S151).

**The `continue_from_retest` handoff (D-41 P1: interface only).**

- FACT and HISTORICAL: `def continue_from_retest(self, parked) -> None` (`signals/signal_processor.py` L2375); its docstring says "Resume a CONFIRMED WAIT_FOR_RETEST candidate. Re-checks kill-switch / market-window / strategy-control / governor ..." (A06's text); its only non-test caller is the monitor at `screening/retest_monitor.py` L187, through the one binding at `main.py` L3786 (P04-S093, P04-S094; P07-S102). The monitor passes one argument, the `ParkedCandidate`, of whose fourteen fields the function reads eight (P04-S095).
- FACT (the retest-derived values): the zone band becomes the structure stop and the break level: for a long `structure_sl = zone_band_low × (1 − retest_sl_buffer_pct/100)` and `break_level = zone_band_high`, the short side mirrored (L2432 to L2442) (P04-S096). The processor's parameter is `retest_sl_buffer_pct: float = 0.2` (L188), and `main.py` passes `retest_sl_buffer_pct=_sr_cfg.sl_buffer_pct` (L3610), the key `sr_detector.sl_buffer_pct` (committed 0.2), not the loader field of the parameter's own name, `sr_detector.retest_sl_buffer_pct` (default 0.3, absent from the committed file), which feeds the V1 proposal (A02 K-22) (P04-S097, P04-S131). The entry estimate is A06's `_retest_entry_estimate(symbol, break_level)`, the quote's last price with the band edge as its fallback (P04-S098).
- FACT (named at the interface only): the retest-derived values reach the bad-structure check (`RETEST_BAD_STRUCTURE`), A09's sizing, the target, A09's reservation, the alert, the evidence record, the placement and the V1 observer (P04-S099). The placement call passes `entry_order_type="MARKET"` (L2553 to L2562), the only non-test call that passes it; the placer and the entry engine default to `"LIMIT"` and pass it to the protocol, which A10 selects; the limit-triple protocol honours MARKET with price 0.0, and the CO protocol takes and ignores it (A10's files) (P04-S100, P04-S121). `signal_trigger_price=parked.trigger_price` also reaches the placer (P04-S101). The function's other call-outs (the kill switch A13, the entry window, the governor, the throttle at L2530 and others) are named with their lines only (P04-S102).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (what A08 receives back): nothing; the function returns `None` on every route and the monitor discards the value; the signal's later statuses are written by the function itself (`PROCESSING` first, with no test of the current status, then `RESERVED`, `PROCESSED_NO_PLACER`, `TIMEOUT`, `PROCESSED`, `REJECTED_<check>` or `PLACEMENT_FAILED`), so `RETEST_CONFIRMED` stands only while the monitor's thread enters the call (P04-S103, P04-S104). INTERPRETATION: the side is decided by the exact test `parked.direction in ("LONG", "BUY")` in the resumption and the audit row, by `_is_long` in `evaluate` and by the side in the diverter; they agree for the two directions the strategy schema admits (P04-S105, P04-S111).

**Threads, locks, clocks and failure behaviour (summary).** The threads (the main thread, A06's worker pool running the diverter, "sr-retest-monitor", A12's end-of-day thread), the one `RLock` that guards only the parked dictionary, the interleavings the code allows, every clock reading and the failure table are in P04 section 9 (P04-S106 to P04-S113) and summarised in section 10 below. INTERPRETATION: the V2 code retries nothing, raises no alert and calls no notifier; its failures are ERROR lines and, at the diverter, a fall-back to normal placement before the commit point or a silent hold after it (P04-S113). FACT (A06 WA-06): no A08 file reads `triggered_at`; the retest clock starts at the divert (P04-S110, P04-S136).

The intent sources are compared in section 12; the points for the AUDIT are P04-AP01 to P04-AP21 (section 15.1), the unknown items P04-U01 to P04-U11 (section 15.2) and the questions P04-R1 to P04-R5 (section 16).

## 7. The corporate-action spot-check and the level-export script

This section covers the two manual scripts of A08, `scripts/sr_corp_action_spotcheck.py` and `scripts/sr_level_export.py` (index, Appendix A). Their broker interface is recorded under the security rules, at the interface only: no credential value, validity or exposure was read, derived or checked, and the token file was not opened (section 1.10; P05-S005). Their collaborators in other topics' files (`main.py`'s helpers, A01; `load_all`, A02; `InstrumentCache`, A05; the limiter, A04) are recorded at the interface only.

Evidence: `A08_SPOTCHECK_AND_EXPORT_SCRIPTS_2026-11-26.md` (P05), which read both scripts and their test in full; the Python, Kite Connect and pydantic behaviour it relies on is current external context read on 26-Nov-2026 (P05-S006 to P05-S008). The question of which line of the export fails first (P07 AP-02; the direction's section 5, "L139 vs L141") was answered by reading only; nothing was run (P05-S059).

**The two scripts.**

- FACT: the spot check is 102 lines with one commit (`dea336ae71c353fd86331a37d0a788e02968aca9`, 2026-06-27, the V1 package's); the export is 203 lines with one commit (`536172cfadfdd28ec9e5f8c00f9d549f0cd57dc2`, 2026-07-12, the V3 shared-engine commit) (P05-S010, P05-S121). No non-test file imports or calls either script; `tests/unit/test_sr_level_export.py` imports the export's pure functions (P05-S016). Neither is scheduled: neither is named in `config/cron_registry.yaml`, under `deploy/` or under `ops/`; each has a manual usage line (P05-S085, P05-S086).
- HISTORICAL: the spot check's purpose: "Purpose (spec C, one-time verification): Confirm whether kite.historical_data() returns split/bonus-ADJUSTED equity candles by default. The detector clusters swing pivots over 6 months — an UNADJUSTED split ... would create a phantom gap and a false zone. Run ON..." (P05-S013). The export's purpose: "Purpose (Task 4 — manual-marking validation): READ-ONLY export of the detector's ANCHOR + SWING levels for a set of symbols + dates, in a flat CSV Rama can visually compare against his MANUAL chart marks. This is the #1 03.01 safeguard: validate auto-detected S&R against the human eye BEFORE it ev..." (P05-S014). INTERPRETATION: "one-time" and "on the VM" are instructions to the operator, not conditions the code enforces (P05-S017).

**`sr_corp_action_spotcheck.py`.**

- FACT: arguments `--symbol` and `--around` (required) and `--window` (integer, default 20 days each side); the window is `ex ± window` days around a naive midnight of the given date, interval `day`; argparse errors end with status 2 (P05-S018 to P05-S020).
- FACT (interface only; security rules): the script builds its own client in `_build_kite` (L33 to L42, called at L60) from the broker session token file under its own repository root, and does not use the trading process's client, `main.py` or any project helper; when the file is absent it prints an ERROR line and returns 1; the read, the parse and the field reads (L39 to L41) have no handler (A04 CL-11, CL-15, AU-05); it reads no configuration file, environment variable, account registry or database (P05-S021, P05-S022, P05-S025, P05-S026). It calls `set_access_token`, `instruments("NSE")` and one `historical_data(..., interval="day")`, each once per run, through no limiter and with no sleep (A04 RL-06) (P05-S023, P05-S024).
- FACT (A05 XC-03): `_token_for` returns the token of the first row of its own download of the NSE dump whose `tradingsymbol` equals the text exactly, with no segment or instrument-type check; a miss prints an ERROR line and exits 1 (P05-S027). INTERPRETATION: for a text carried by several rows the token is that of the first such row; what the dump held in the legacy period is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (P05-S028).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: with fewer than two rows the script exits 1; otherwise it finds the largest consecutive close-to-close deviation in the rows as received and prints "VERDICT: ADJUSTED (continuous)" when `abs(worst_ratio − 1.0) < 0.15` and the UNADJUSTED text otherwise; the exit status is 0 for both verdicts (P05-S029 to P05-S031, P05-S033). INTERPRETATION: the `--around` date only places the window; any consecutive pair with a 15 % or larger step gives the UNADJUSTED text, at whatever date; an unadjusted factor between 0.85 and 1.15 gives the ADJUSTED text when no other pair deviates by 0.15 or more (P05-S032).
- FACT: output is standard output only: no file, database, log, alert or document is written, and "note the result in SYSTEM_MAP" is an instruction to the person running it; the file has no `try`, so an exception from the token-file read, the client or a request ends the run with a traceback (P05-S034, P05-S035). Nothing consumes the result: no configuration key, row or code path takes it, and no A08 code adjusts candles (P05-S036; P03-S183). FACT and current external context: the broker's historical-data page says nothing about adjustment; its support article says the Kite charts' historical data are adjusted for corporate actions; developer-forum replies by one account whose role is not shown (not documentation) say, in July 2025 and on 29 September and 1 October (no year shown), that historical data are adjusted, that no unadjusted series is provided and that candle values change when later adjustments are applied. INTERPRETATION: these pages describe the broker's service as stated there, not what it returned to the legacy system; whether the legacy period's candles were adjusted stays UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (P05-S038; A05 HA-07).
- FACT and HISTORICAL (A05 HA-07; entry A05-32): three files name the script (the script, `PATHS.md` L422, `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` L196); no document, commit message or status list records a run or a result; `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` holds no result (P05-S039 to P05-S045). Whether the check was ever run, and what it printed, is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (P05-S046; P05-U01).

**`sr_level_export.py`: the analysis function and where it stops (L137, L139, L141).**

- FACT: `_make_real_analyze_fn(interval, orb_window)` (L127 to L175) is one straight-line block with no branch and no `try`: function-local imports (L130 to L137, among them `from main import _build_market_data_kite, _make_sr_fetch_fn, _parse_hhmm` at L137), then the set-up L139 to L148, the configuration copy L150 to L155 and the inner `analyze_fn` (P05-S047, P05-S048). Importing `main` executes `main.py`'s top level and its 74 imports (A01 C40) (P05-S049, P05-S122).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (L139): `load_all(str(ROOT / "config"))` passes a `str` to `load_all(config_dir: Path = Path("config"))`, whose loop computes `config_dir / filename` at `core/config_loader.py` L2481; the annotation is not enforced, and `str` defines no division, so the interpreter raises `TypeError` there; neither the function nor `main` handles it, so the run ends with a traceback before any instrument file is read, any client or limiter built, any broker call made or any CSV written (P05-S051 to P05-S053).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (L141 to L145, as written, not reached): L141 `InstrumentCache(str(path))` would raise `AttributeError` iterating the characters of a string; L142 calls the class method `load` with no argument and would raise `TypeError`; L143 `_build_market_data_kite(is_paper=False, kite_client=None)` takes the live branch and returns `None`; L144 would build a limiter of the script's own; past them every request would receive `[]` from the closure, with no broker call (P05-S054 to P05-S058; A04 CL-12 for the export's path).
- INTERPRETATION (the answer; P07 AP-02): at the baseline the first failing line of the export's real wiring is either (a) an import, the module-level L37 or one of L130 to L137, if one of them raises in the environment where the script is run, which cannot be established without running it (UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED, P05-S050); or (b) otherwise L139, with a `TypeError` raised inside `load_all` at `core/config_loader.py` L2481. L141's `AttributeError` and L142's `TypeError` are what those lines do taken alone, but neither can be the first failure in any environment, since L139 precedes them in the same straight-line block and raises whenever it is reached (P05-S059). HISTORICAL: `load_all`, `InstrumentCache` and the helper had these forms at the script's own commit, so the wiring has stopped at the same place at every commit of its history (P05-S060). FACT: A01 C38b records L141, A02 L-47 and the handoff row L554 record L139, and A05 records "stops at L139" "(if its imports succeed)"; no earlier record is changed (P05-S061).
- FACT (as written): the script never calls `setup_logging`, and `get_logger`'s docstring says handlers are not attached there (whether a module imported at L137 configures logging was not traced; INTERPRETATION, P05-S062); the session bounds come from the configured `trading_hours` (P05-S063); the copy is `app_config.system.sr_detector.model_copy(update={"enabled": True, "intraday_anchors_enabled": True, "anchor_intraday_interval": interval, "orb_window_minutes": orb_window})`, which does not validate (current external context, P02-S160) (P05-S064); each `analyze_fn(symbol, on_date)` builds a new detector through `build_sr_detector(..., store=None, mode="live", now_fn=lambda: as_of, ...)`, fetches the daily series for the reference price, builds an export `Candidate` and calls `analyze` only (P05-S065, P05-S066). `analyze` never returns `None`; a fetch failure returns a `FETCH_FAILED` analysis with empty anchors and swings (P05-S067).

**`sr_level_export.py`: the export, the clock and the output.**

- FACT: `main` takes `--symbols`, `--dates`, `--interval` (default `5minute`, no `choices`), `--orb-window` (default 15) and `--out`; it builds the analysis function, runs `export_levels` (each symbol, then each date, sequentially), converts to CSV and writes the file, then prints one count line (P05-S069 to P05-S071). The CSV has ten columns (`symbol`, `date`, `layer`, `level_type`, `role_timeframe`, `kind`, `price_low`, `price_high`, `confidence`, `confidence_class`); layer A rows are the prior-day levels, the round numbers, the VWAP and the opening range; layer B rows are the swing zones by role (P05-S072 to P05-S074). Not exported are the reference price, the ORB window, a swing's score, touches and timeframe, and every other analysis field (`structure_status`, flags, note, the nearest zones, the retest proposal, the evidence) (P05-S075). Every row carries `ANCHOR_ONLY` (P05-S076). INTERPRETATION: a missing key would be written as "", an extra key ignored (P05-S077).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (the clock; A02 K-02): `as_of = datetime.combine(date.fromisoformat(on_date), time(15, 30))`, a naive literal 15:30, is the detector's and the fetcher's clock and the candidate's time; the configured `market_open` and `market_close` are used only for the anchors' session bounds; at the committed values the literal equals `market_close`; the requested date is checked against neither the trading calendar nor the current date (P05-S078 to P05-S080, P05-S094).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: the CSV goes to `--out` or `reports/output/sr_levels.csv`, written only after every symbol and date has been processed; the file has no `try` (P05-S081, P05-S082). The script places no order, sizes nothing, opens no database and writes no `sr_detector_results` row, as its "READ-ONLY export" says, while it writes the CSV (P05-S083). INTERPRETATION: "it runs the same pure detection path" holds for the analysis functions; the inputs differ (a constant 15:30 clock, `enabled` and the anchors forced on, one detector per symbol and date, no store) (P05-S084).

**The export's role in the legacy records.**

- HISTORICAL: `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` L233 describes the export as working ("READ-ONLY; dumps anchors + swings ... to a flat CSV Rama compares against his manual chart marks") (P05-S087, P05-S088). The committed switch `intraday_anchors_enabled: false` carries the comment "Flip true AFTER the sr_level_export manual-marking validation passes", and the loader's comment says the same (P05-S089); the legacy pending lists record the calibration and the manual zone-marking pass as open (P05-S090). INTERPRETATION: the committed comments make the manual-marking validation, made from this script's CSV, the condition for turning the intraday anchors on, while by reading the script could not produce the CSV at the baseline (P05-S091). FACT: no code of the trading process runs, imports or reads the export or its CSV (P05-S092).

The intent sources are compared in section 12; the points for the AUDIT are P05-AP01 to P05-AP17 (section 15.1), the unknown items P05-U01 to P05-U07 (section 15.2) and the questions P05-R1 to P05-R3 (section 16).

## 8. Configuration read by A08: committed values, defaults across layers, effective values

This section records every configuration key that reaches A08 code, its committed value, its loader default and every reader's fallback. The configuration file and the loader are A02's and are cited for key names, committed values, defaults and validators only. **Every committed value is FACT about the file only; every production-effective value is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED** (Document 06, section 4.2; P06-S006; P06-U01).

Evidence: `A08_CONFIGURATION_WIRING_AND_CONSUMER_INTERFACES_2026-11-26.md` (P06), section 2 (the key-by-key table, one statement per field, P06-S011 to P06-S056) and section 3, with the value layers of P01 (P01-S069) and P02 (P02-S045 to P02-S047) and the V2 keys of P04 (P04-S019, P04-S034, P04-S128).

**The model and the file.**

- FACT: `SystemConfig.sr_detector: SRDetectorConfig = Field(default_factory=SRDetectorConfig)` and `SystemConfig.structure_exit: StructureExitConfig = Field(default_factory=StructureExitConfig)` (`core/config_loader.py` L2012, L2017), so either section may be absent and is then built from its field defaults; `SRDetectorConfig` (L1153 to L1255) has `extra="forbid"`, 45 fields, every one with a default, and three validators (the allowed timeframes, `require_confidence` and `anchor_intraday_interval`, by set membership) (P06-S009). The trading process's `load_all` reads exactly eight files, with no merge step and no overlay file ("CL5 — No env-var interpolation, no includes. yaml.safe_load() only.") (P06-S007). The A08 block is `config/system_config.yaml` L508 to L545; `structure_exit_enabled` sits in the `structure_exit:` block (L634) (P06-S010, P06-S056; inventory, section 4).
- FACT (the three S&R switches): `sr_detector.enabled` committed `true` (L509; loader `False`), the V1 observer; `sr_detector.wait_for_retest_enabled` committed `false` (L529; loader `False`), the V2 retest path; `structure_exit.structure_exit_enabled` committed `false` (L634; loader `False`), A11's structure exit, read by `main.py` L3427, by `core/config_auditor.py` L224 (rule A4) and by `orders/structure_exit_manager.py` L127 to L129; and `sr_detector.intraday_anchors_enabled` committed `false` (L542), the V1 detector's anchors (P06-S011, P06-S040, P06-S052, P06-S056).

**The committed values and the readers (P06 section 2).**

- FACT (structure and zones): `timeframes` `["day", "60minute", "30minute"]` (read by the zone builder, so by every holder of the knobs, the A18 consumers included); `lookback_days` 180 and `cache_ttl_sec` 1800 (the V1 fetcher, the structure fetcher, the export; the regime fetcher's TTL too); `default_pivot_n` 5; `cluster_pct` 0.5; `merge_pct` 0.4; `band_buffer_pct` 0.1; the six weights 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.5; `t_high` 5.0; `t_med` 3.0 (P06-S012 to P06-S019, P06-S027 to P06-S034).
- FACT (the V1 flags and the anchors): `entry_proximity_pct` 1.0 and `volume_surge_mult` 1.5; `anchor_intraday_interval` `5minute`, `anchor_lookback_days` 1, `orb_window_minutes` 15 (the export's copy overrides `enabled`, `intraday_anchors_enabled`, `anchor_intraday_interval` and `orb_window_minutes`) (P06-S023, P06-S024, P06-S052 to P06-S055).
- FACT (the V2 path and its infrastructure): `near_zone_buffer_pct` 0.3, `require_confidence` `HIGH` (read by the diverter); `retest_timeout_sec` 1800, `retest_max_away_pct` 1.0, `confirm_strong_close_frac` 0.6, `breakout_margin_pct` 0.0 (read by `main.py` into `RetestParams`); `onem_lookback_days` 2, `zone_cache_ttl_sec` 1800, `zone_rewarm_margin_sec` 300, `retest_poll_interval_sec` 20; `sl_buffer_pct` 0.2 (read by `main.py` L3610 into the processor's `retest_sl_buffer_pct` and by the diverter for the parked stop) (P06-S040 to P06-S051).
- FACT (eleven loader fields not in the file, whose only source on the configured path is the loader default): `pivot_n_by_tf` `{}`, `volume_bins` 24, `volume_node_frac` 0.7, `breakout_avg_window` 20, `weak_breakout_frac` 0.25, `retest_sl_buffer_pct` 0.3, `touch_cap` 4, `recency_window_days` 90.0, `recency_min_factor` 0.0, `max_queue` 256 and `max_zones_logged` 12; in the V1 flag step `weak_breakout_frac`, `breakout_avg_window` and `retest_sl_buffer_pct` decide `WEAK_BREAKOUT`, the volume average and the proposed stop (P06-S057, P06-S061). The file's only `max_queue` key (512, L616) is the `v3_chain:` section's (P06-S038, P06-S067).
- FACT: every one of the 45 fields has at least one non-test reader, every name read from the `sr_detector` object is a field, and no key lacks a reader (P06-S057, P06-S059, P06-S060). FACT (units, A02 K-21): every `_pct` key is applied as a percentage, divided by 100 at its point of use, each against its own reference quantity (the previous pivot, the group's mean centre, the band's centre, the zone's centre or the band edge); the `_frac` and `_mult` keys are fractions or multiples; the time keys are seconds or calendar days (P06-S058, P06-S140; P02-S133, P03-S177, P04-S153).

**Defaults across layers and name collisions.**

- FACT: for every field the reader's fallback equals the loader default; the one dataclass default that differs is `ScoringParams.band_buffer_pct = 0.0` against 0.1 in the builder fallback, the loader and the file, which applies only where `ScoringParams` is built directly (the unit tests) (P06-S064; P01-S069, P01-AP09). The file differs from the loader default for one key only, `enabled` (`true` against `False`) (P06-S065). INTERPRETATION: on the configured path the file decides only the V1 switch; the other 33 committed keys restate the defaults (P06-S065).
- FACT (three stop-buffer keys; A02 K-22): `sr_detector.retest_sl_buffer_pct` (0.3, loader only) feeds the V1 proposal; `sr_detector.sl_buffer_pct` (0.2) feeds the divert's parked stop and, through the processor parameter named `retest_sl_buffer_pct`, the resumption's stop; `structure_exit.sl_buffer_pct` (0.2, L635, comment "match Phase A") feeds A11's structure exit (P06-S026, P06-S051, P06-S066; P04-S097, P03-S178).
- FACT (two queue bounds; A02 K-23): the detector's queue is `max_queue` (loader 256); the warmer's is its constructor default 512, which no key reaches (P06-S062, P06-S067; P02-S076). Constructor values that no key reaches: the warmer's queue size, idle sleep and `enabled`; the monitor's interval `"minute"` and `enabled`; the two `detector_version` strings `"snr-v1"` and `"snr-v2"` (P06-S062).
- FACT (one key read for another section's object; A02 K-24): `sr_detector.cache_ttl_sec` is the regime fetcher's cache TTL (`main.py` L3523), and the zone knobs and scoring parameters built from the `sr_detector` section are handed to the V3 chain (`main.py` L3686 to L3687, comment "REUSE the sr_detector zone knobs (no duplicate tuning)") and to two of the three PB-01 consumers (P06-S063). FACT: no range or sign check exists for any numeric field; the readers cast without checks (P06-S009; P06-AP07).
- FACT and HISTORICAL: the loader's module docstring says "Does not fill in defaults for missing keys (CL3: extra="forbid", all required)", while both A08 sections give every field a default (A02 L-04, unchanged; P06-S068). INTERPRETATION: several committed integer values reach `float` fields; every receiver casts with `float(...)`, so no A08 result depends on the stored type (current external context, the pydantic conversion table, read 26-Nov-2026) (P06-S069).

**Effective values.** UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED: the deployed value of every key above and of `structure_exit_enabled`, and so which switch row of section 9 the process was in on any day; only the committed file survives, legacy documents' statements of the deployed values are claims, not evidence, and the loader has no overlay that the repository would show; A07 XS-04 records Rama's statement (RS-23) that tracked configuration files were at times changed directly on the VM (P06-U01; P06-S185, P06-S186). The arming commit records a manual change of `enabled` on the machine before it was committed (P03-S214; P03-U02).

## 9. Wiring, start and shutdown; switch combinations

`main.py` is A01's file; this section records at the interface what it constructs for A08, under which switch, with which values, in which order, what a failure leaves, and how the A08 objects are started and stopped (D-41, P3). The internals of what is built are sections 3 to 7.

Evidence: P06 sections 4 to 6 (P06-S070 to P06-S091), with P02, P03 and P04 for the objects' own start and stop.

**The switches, the guard and the closure.**

- FACT: `_sr_cfg = app_config.system.sr_detector`; `_v1_on = _sr_cfg.enabled`; `_v2_on = getattr(_sr_cfg, "wait_for_retest_enabled", False)`; `_struct_exit_on = getattr(_struct_exit_cfg, "structure_exit_enabled", False)` (L3421 to L3427), each read once; three A18 switches also build A08 objects: `_regime_on`, `_v3_chain_on` and `_watchlist_on` (committed `false`, `"shadow"` and `true`) (P06-S070, P06-S071).
- FACT: before any A08 object, only when `_struct_exit_on is True`, rule A4 of `core/config_auditor.py` (structure exit on and any enabled strategy with `trailing_sl_enabled`) ends the boot with return code 3 (L3428 to L3443) (P06-S072).
- FACT: the closure `_sr_fetch_fn = _make_sr_fetch_fn(_md_kite, rate_limiter)` is built once when any of the six switches is on (L3479 to L3487), with a WARNING when the market-data handle is `None`; in Live `_build_market_data_kite` returns the trading process's own client (L404 to L405) (P06-S073; A01 B-11). FACT (A01 B-11): A08 code never receives the handle; every `OhlcFetcher` receives only the closure (P06-S139; P02-S132).

**The constructions, block by block (committed switch values in brackets; values only).**

- FACT: (1) **V1 observer** (L3489 to L3508; `_v1_on` [`true`]): `build_sr_detector(config=_sr_cfg, fetch_fn=_sr_fetch_fn, instrument_cache=..., store=store, logger=..., mode=mode_label, session_open=market_windows.market_open, session_close=market_windows.market_close)` with no `now_fn`, then `start()`; on an exception ERROR "sr_detector wiring failed (continuing without it)" and `sr_detector = None`. (2) **Regime** (A18; [`false`]). (3) **V2 infrastructure** (L3542 to L3572; `_v2_on or _struct_exit_on` [`false`, `false`]): the knobs, the scoring parameters, `ZoneCache`, the structure fetcher (registered with telemetry), `ZoneWarmer`; on an exception both the warmer and the cache become `None`. (4) **Signal processor** (A06; always; L3575 to L3623): `sr_detector=sr_detector`, `zone_warmer=zone_warmer`, `retest_sl_buffer_pct=_sr_cfg.sl_buffer_pct`. (5) **V3 chain** (A18; on) and (6) **PB-01 watchlist** (A18; [`true`]), each with its own `OhlcFetcher` and the reused `sr_detector` knobs. (7) **V2 retest** (L3765 to L3802; `_v2_on and zone_warmer is not None and _v2_zone_cache is not None` [`false`]): the one-minute fetcher, `RetestParams`, `RetestMonitor(on_confirm=signal_processor.continue_from_retest, ...)`, `RetestDiverter`, `set_retest_diverter`, `eod.set_retest_monitor`, `rehydrate()`, the warmer's and the monitor's starts; its `except` sets only `retest_monitor = None`. (8) **Structure exit** (A11; L3804 to L3837; `_struct_exit_on` [`false`]): logs an ERROR and does not start when the cache or the warmer is missing; otherwise starts the warmer (idempotent) and builds and starts the manager with the zone cache (P06-S074).
- FACT: the V1 detector and the diverter receive the whole `_sr_cfg` object; the warmer and the monitor receive values read by `main.py`; the processor receives `sl_buffer_pct` as `retest_sl_buffer_pct`; the structure exit receives `_struct_exit_cfg` (P06-S075). `register_constructed("ohlc_fetchers")` follows the five fetcher constructions in `main.py` (L3524, L3563, L3683, L3733, L3778) and none for the V1 detector's fetcher (A05 XC-03) (P06-S076). The diverter and the zone cache are not passed to `_shutdown` and run no thread (P06-S077). The end-of-day object, built at L3300 before every A08 block, receives the monitor only through L3794 (P06-S078).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION (what a failure leaves; A01 C37): a V1 failure leaves `sr_detector = None` and a no-op `_sr_observe`; a V2 infrastructure failure leaves both names `None`, so the V2 retest block is skipped and the structure-exit block logs its ERROR; a V2 retest failure after L3793 leaves a bound diverter and the end-of-day reference while `_shutdown` gets `None`, and before L3796 a built, injected, never-started warmer when the structure exit is off; a structure-exit failure leaves the warmer started; the V2 infrastructure block counts its fetcher before building the warmer (P06-S079, P06-S138; P04-S091, P02-S078). INTERPRETATION: the processor is built after the V1, regime and V2 infrastructure blocks and before the V3, PB-01 and V2 retest blocks, so it receives the warmer whenever the infrastructure block succeeded; the diverter is bound only by L3793 (P06-S080).

**Start order and the boot steps after it (A01 C13).**

- FACT: the starts that involve A08 objects, in line order: `sr_detector.start()` (L3504; "sr-detector"); the A18 starts whose threads call A08 fetchers (L3536, L3694, L3748, L3749); `retest_monitor.rehydrate()` (L3795); `zone_warmer.start()` (L3796; "sr-zone-warmer"); `retest_monitor.start()` (L3797; "sr-retest-monitor"); `zone_warmer.start()` again in the structure-exit block (L3816; returns at once when a worker exists); `structure_exit_manager.start()` (L3832, A11's). After the constructions come `eod.post_wire_init()` (L3866, A12's restart recovery, which can call `clear_all()`), the startup reconcile (L3880), the kill-switch check, which on an active switch returns 1 (L3894 to L3899), and `signal_processor.start()` (L3974), after which the dispatcher can call `_warm_zones` and the workers `_sr_observe` and the divert (P06-S081, P06-S137).
- INTERPRETATION: the detector's and the warmer's workers only wait on empty queues before L3974, so they make no fetch, write or broker call before then; the monitor's worker polls its rehydrated set from 20 s after L3797 (committed `retest_poll_interval_sec`) and could by the code's structure hand a candidate to `continue_from_retest` before the reconcile and the processor's start, while at the committed wiring the evaluation raises first (section 6) (P06-S082; P03-S036, P02-S131, P04-S092). FACT and INTERPRETATION: when the kill check returns 1, `_shutdown` is not called, and the started A08 daemons end with the interpreter (current external context: "Daemon threads are abruptly stopped at shutdown") (P06-S083).

**Shutdown order (`_shutdown`, L1599 to L1830, called at L4252 to L4277).**

- FACT: `_shutdown(..., sr_detector=..., zone_warmer=..., retest_monitor=..., structure_exit_manager=..., ...)` (L4266 to L4276), with parameters defaulting to `None` (P06-S084). The order of the steps that concern A08: `_shutdown_event.set()` first (L1629, "so rate limiter aborts immediately"); then the kill-switch flatten drain, the effect census and the webhook receiver's stop; `signal_proc.stop()` (L1689 to L1692); `sr_detector.stop()` (L1695 to L1699, "after signal_processor so no new candidates are enqueued"); the A18 workers and A09's allocator; `retest_monitor.stop()` (L1731 to L1735); `structure_exit_manager.stop()` (L1737 to L1741); `zone_warmer.stop()` (L1742 to L1746); then the entry gate, the order-side objects, `live_feed.disconnect()` (L1788), `candle_store.stop()` (L1792) and, last, `store.close()` (L1828); each A08 stop is in its own `try` that logs ERROR and continues, and runs only when the object is not `None` (P06-S085).
- INTERPRETATION: the processor stops before the detector, so no new candidate is offered while the detector drains; the cache's two readers stop before the warmer that fills it; each A08 `stop` joins its worker for 2.0 s without checking the outcome and does not interrupt a fetch, so a worker that outlives its join can still be running at `store.close()` (the detector's write; the monitor's release or callback) (P06-S086; P03-S033, P02-S089, P04-S090).

**Switch combinations and the A08 objects they produce (P06-S088; V1 = `sr_detector.enabled`, V2 = `wait_for_retest_enabled`, SE = `structure_exit_enabled`; each row assumes no construction raises and the A4 guard finds no trailing strategy).**

| Row | V1 | V2 | SE | A08 objects built | Started | Handed to the processor | Handed to the end-of-day object | Handed to `_shutdown` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | off | off | off | none (the closure only if an A18 switch is on) | none | `sr_detector=None`, `zone_warmer=None`; no diverter | none | all four `None` |
| 2 (committed values) | on | off | off | `SRDetector` with its own `OhlcFetcher` | "sr-detector" | the detector; `zone_warmer=None`; no diverter | none | `sr_detector` only |
| 3 | off | on | off | `ZoneCache`, structure fetcher, `ZoneWarmer`; one-minute fetcher, `RetestParams`, `RetestMonitor`, `RetestDiverter` | rehydrate; "sr-zone-warmer"; "sr-retest-monitor" | the warmer; the diverter bound | the monitor | `zone_warmer`, `retest_monitor` |
| 4 | off | off | on | `ZoneCache`, structure fetcher, `ZoneWarmer`; `StructureExitManager` (A11) | "sr-zone-warmer"; the manager | the warmer; no diverter | none | `zone_warmer`, `structure_exit_manager` |
| 5 | off | on | on | rows 3 and 4 | as row 3, then the idempotent warmer start and the manager | as row 3 | the monitor | `zone_warmer`, `retest_monitor`, `structure_exit_manager` |
| 6 | on | on | off | rows 2 and 3 | rows 2 and 3 | the detector, the warmer, the diverter | the monitor | `sr_detector`, `zone_warmer`, `retest_monitor` |
| 7 | on | off | on | rows 2 and 4 | rows 2 and 4 | the detector, the warmer; no diverter | none | `sr_detector`, `zone_warmer`, `structure_exit_manager` |
| 8 | on | on | on | rows 2 and 5 | rows 2 and 5 | the detector, the warmer, the diverter | the monitor | all four |

- FACT (the table, P06-S087, P06-S088, derived from `main.py` L3480, L3491, L3549, L3769, L3807 to L3811 and L3436). FACT: `intraday_anchors_enabled` builds no object and changes only what the V1 detector fetches and writes; the A18 switches build their own fetchers and reuse the `sr_detector` knobs independently of V1, V2 and SE; the export script builds its own detector, whatever the file says, in a separate process (P06-S089).
- INTERPRETATION: at the committed values the process is in row 2 for A08's own objects, plus the V3 and PB-01 fetchers of A18: three `OhlcFetcher` objects over the closure, no zone cache, warmer, monitor or diverter, and no monitor in the end-of-day object; the processor's `_warm_zones` and divert tests are no-ops; legacy documents state the same deployed values (HISTORICAL), which is a document's statement, not surviving runtime evidence (P06-S090; P06-U01). INTERPRETATION: row 4 (the structure exit alone) starts the warmer and feeds it only from signal arrivals, so the cache holds zones only for symbols that had a signal since the process started, while the structure exit reads it for open positions; rows 6, 7 and 8 run the V1 detector and the warmer over two separate fetchers with separate caches for the same intervals and lookback (P06-S091; P02-S159).

## 10. Clocks, threads, locks, retries, failure, missing or invalid data and restart behaviour across A08

This section answers the direction's item A.6 in one place: a summary by concern across the six areas of A08, citing the parts' own summaries (P01 section 7; P02 section 7; P03 sections 4, 7 and 8; P04 section 9; P05 sections 3 to 6; P06 sections 4 and 5). It adds no new fact. Each cell summarises the statements it cites and keeps their label: a cell marked (I) rests on statements labelled INTERPRETATION (code reading of inputs or interleavings the code was never shown to meet, with the Python or library semantics read as current external context); unmarked cells are FACT. Every runtime occurrence is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED (section 1.6).

| Concern | Calculation core (P01) | Fetch, zone builder, cache, warmer (P02) | V1 observer and backfill (P03) | V2 retest path (P04) | Scripts (P05) | Wiring (P06) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Threads | none of its own; runs on its callers' threads (P01-S010) | "sr-zone-warmer" (daemon); `enqueue` on A06's "sp-dispatcher"; `ZoneCache.get` on A06's workers and A11's candle thread (P02-S091) | "sr-detector" (daemon), one serialized worker (P03-S030, P03-S034); the backfill in its own process (P03-S124) | "sr-retest-monitor" (daemon); the diverter on A06's workers; `clear_all` on A12's thread (P04-S106) | each script in its own process, sequential (P05-S024, P05-S071) | three A08 daemons, started during construction before the reconcile and the processor's start (P06-S081, P06-S137) |
| Locks | none (P01-S010) | four locks, none taken while another is held and none held during a fetch, limiter wait, broker call, scoring run or log call (P02-S092) | none of its own; the queue's internal lock (P03-S034, P03-S039) | one `RLock` guarding only the parked dictionary; `has_symbol` and `register` are separate critical sections (P04-S107; (I) P04-S108) | none (P05-S024) | — |
| Clocks | no clock; the caller's `now`; a `None` clock gives the full recency vote (P01-S010; (I) P01-S091); time-zone stripping when one side is naive ((I) P01-S090) | injected `now_ist` only, the wall clock in IST; no monotonic or market clock, against the comment "monotonic" (P02-S029, P02-S093, P02-S144) | `now_ist` by default; aware IST stamps in the row (P03-S018, P03-S085); the backfill selects by SQLite `date(ts)`, the UTC date of the stamp ((I) P03-S116), and falls back to the host's local date ((I) P03-S125) | naive divert time against the aware `now_ist` of the monitor: the elapsed-time subtraction raises `TypeError` at every evaluation at the committed wiring ((I) P04-S070); every clock reading tabled (P04-S109) | the export's constant naive 15:30 (P05-S078); the spot check's naive midnight window (P05-S020) | `time_authority.now_ist` passed to every A08 object built in `main.py` (P06-S117) |
| Retries and pacing | — | none on the fetch path; the closure's `acquire("historical")` is the only pacing; a failed interval is asked again only at the next miss (P02-S039) | none; up to three historical calls per candidate through the shared limiter ((I) P03-S105) | none; one historical request per parked candidate per poll ((I) P04-S077, P04-S113) | the spot check paces nothing; the export's own limiter is not reached (P05-S024, P05-S057) | the closure built once over the process's limiter (P06-S073) |
| Failure handling | nothing caught; exceptions go to the callers (P01-S097) | one WARNING text for every fetch exception; two paths raise despite "never raise" ((I) P02-S036); the failure table (P02-S094) | `observe` never raises for a `Candidate` ((I) P03-S103); an analysis exception gives one ERROR and no row ((I) P03-S055); a write failure one ERROR (P03-S086) | no alert, notifier or retry; ERROR lines; a fall-back to normal placement before the commit point, a silent hold after it ((I) P04-S113); the failure table (P04-S112) | no `try` in either script; a traceback ends the run (P05-S035, P05-S082) | an optional block's failure is logged and leaves partial state ((I) P06-S079) |
| Missing or invalid data | `from_kite` validates nothing; NaN suppresses pivots; NaN or infinities raise in two functions; mixed time forms raise in two `max` calls ((I) P01-S097, P01-S017, P01-S018) | a failed and an empty interval are both absent ((I) P02-S016); partial results stored like complete ones ((I) P02-S086); the prior day by list position ((I) P02-S048) | a fetch failure gives a `FETCH_FAILED` row; a partial fetch is not recorded (P03-S044; (I) P03-S056); a NULL `created_at` loses the row ((I) P03-S092) | no candles: the fold stays in progress or times out ((I) P04-S066, P04-S076); a NaN or infinite entry matches no zone (P04-S039) | a fetch failure leaves no row and no marker in the export (P05-S067, P05-S075) | — |
| Order and duplicates | only the pivots depend on order; nothing sorts or de-duplicates ((I) P01-S094, P01-S095) | rows passed in the closure's order; no sort, de-duplication or gap test (P02-S026) | the "breakout candle" is the last list element ((I) P03-S053) | the fold uses list order and counts duplicates ((I) P04-S028) | the spot check uses the rows as received (P05-S029) | — |
| Still-forming candles and as-of time | a forming last bar cannot be a pivot but decides its neighbours ((I) P01-S096) | no as-of truncation or completeness test ((I) P02-S024) | the windows end when the worker reaches the candidate, not at the placement stamp ((I) P03-S057) | a forming minute can cause an immediate terminal result ((I) P04-S067, P04-S068) | the export's windows end at 15:30 of the date, which can be after the run ((I) P05-S080) | — |
| Restart | — | the two caches live in memory only, are never pruned and are lost at a restart (P02-S032, P02-S073) | the queue lives in memory only; queued candidates are lost ((I) P03-S216); a backfill re-run considers only NULL rows ((I) P03-S123) | `rehydrate` restores every `retest_state` row, whatever its date or status ((I) P04-S081); a failed release can lead to a second resumption after a restart ((I) P04-S082, P04-S083) | — | the kill check can end a boot without `_shutdown` ((I) P06-S083) |
| Shutdown | — | `stop` joins for 2.0 s; a running warm can finish later ((I) P02-S088, P02-S089) | `stop` joins for 2.0 s; queued candidates are dropped ((I) P03-S031, P03-S033) | `stop` joins for 2.0 s; a poll or callback in progress is not interrupted ((I) P04-S089, P04-S090) | — | the order of the A08 stops and `store.close()` last ((I) P06-S085, P06-S086) |
| Logging, counters, alerts | no logging (P01-S010) | log lines only; no counter, alert or stored record (P02-S094) | the telemetry counter after each successful insert (P03-S086); the backfill prints one line (P03-S136) | log lines only (P04-S078, P04-S113) | the spot check prints only; the export's logger has no handlers (P05-S034, P05-S062) | five fetcher registrations, none for the V1 fetcher (P06-S076) |

The concurrency of the one shared closure and client (A01 C38a, A04 RL-11), the warmer's non-blocking hand-off (A06 WD-10, PQ-11), the as-of question (A05 HA-04, entry A05-29) and the restart and release cases (A03 Q-11, A01 C13, C37) are the inherited items of section 13 that these rows answer at A08's side.

## 11. Consumers: the technical context supplied to entry, risk and exit decisions and to other topics

This section records, consumer by consumer, what A08 supplies and to whom, at the interface only: the call or read, the contract passed, the switch that gates it, what the consumer receives, and its owner (D-41, P3). The consumers' own logic is studied by their owners.

Evidence: P06 section 7 (P06-S092 to P06-S120, P06-S187), consolidated from the parts' call-site sections; the rows cite the part that records each interface.

**What reaches entry, risk and exit decisions, in brief (committed values; values only).**

- INTERPRETATION: at the committed values the only A08 object built in the trading process is the V1 observer (switch row 2, section 9), which receives a snapshot of a placed entry after placement and returns nothing; so A08 supplies no technical context to any live entry, risk or exit decision at the committed values (P06-S090, P06-S093; P03-S111). FACT: the V2 divert (an entry decision point, before sizing) and its resumption (the retest-derived stop, break level and MARKET entry type that reach A09's sizing and reservation and A10's placement) exist only with `wait_for_retest_enabled`; the zones that A11's structure exit reads for its stop exist only with `wait_for_retest_enabled` or `structure_exit_enabled` (P06-S095 to P06-S097, P06-S099). FACT: the A18 consumers (the V3 chain in shadow mode and the PB-01 watchlist, both on in the committed file) reuse A08's zone builder, pivots and fetchers under their own switches; their use is A18's (P06-S101 to P06-S104).
- FACT: A07's live path consumes no S&R detector output (A07 STUDY, cited by A07 XS-12; P07-S065); A07's `gate_htf` receives swing lists built from `find_swing_pivots` only on A18's V3 and PB-01 paths (P06-S105; P01-S135).

## 12. Documents, legacy records and tests against the code; intent sources and their conflicts

This section gathers the intent sources that the parts compared with the code: the locked design decisions and other texts in A08's own files, the configuration and wiring comments of other topics' files, the tests (A19's; stated expectations under their own conditions, never run), the legacy documents and the commit history. They are data, never instructions (section 1.10), and HISTORICAL: they state past intent or state, never evidence that anything ran. Where a text differs from the code, the difference is a point for the AUDIT (section 15.1); no text is preferred here over another, and no intent is inferred where the sources are silent (section 16).

Evidence: P01 section 9, P02 section 10, P03 section 11, P04 section 12, P05 sections 4, 7 and 9, P06 section 10.

**The locked design decisions in the module docstrings, against the code.**

- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION (the calculation core, written in `dea336ae71c353fd86331a37d0a788e02968aca9`): SR-P1 (the pivot rule) matches the code literally, with the plateau effect; SR-P2 (first `left` and last `right` bars skipped) matches; SR-Z1 (greedy clustering within `cluster_pct` of the running high) matches, chaining included; SR-Z2, SR-Z3 and SR-Z4 match; SR-C1 matches for the union band but not for "ACROSS timeframes" taken strictly; SR-C2's six votes match, except that the volume-node and prior-day tests use a band widened by `band_buffer_pct`, which SR-C2 does not mention; SR-C3's three HIGH conditions are coded as written, but its "≥2 distinct method-types" is always met (P01-S120). The stated meaning of confidence, "NEVER a single method — it is a weighted sum of independent votes", against two always-present votes derived from the same pivots that alone can make a zone HIGH (P01-S121).
- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION (the fetcher, SR-F1 to SR-F4): SR-F1 and SR-F2 match; SR-F3's key "(symbol, interval) with a TTL" matches as written and was written when every call of one fetcher asked a window of one length; SR-F4's "nothing propagates into the pipeline" is not literal for two paths (P02-S142, P02-S155, P02-S157; P02-S036).
- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION (the retest state machine, `retest_confirm.py` L20 to L31): each stated transition is what the code does; "idempotent and restart-safe ... re-seeing candles never mis-advances" holds for the pure function, while the stored snapshot can move back after a fetch with no candles (P04-S161, P04-S162).

**Docstrings and comments in A08's files that differ from the code (each also an AUDIT point).**

- HISTORICAL: in the core, "Pure data — no behaviour" (`models.py` L5), "a bar may yield both a HIGH and a LOW pivot only in degenerate single-bar windows", "A price BAND (never a single line)", "everything here is already in hand at _emit_signal_alert" and "All CONFIG-tuned (visual-review phase)" while every committed weight equals the code default (P01-S122; P01-AP16). In the fetcher and caches, "fetch the 3 timeframe windows", "cache within the session", "fetched_at_monotonic_dt", "never raises" and "returns None on any failure"; in `zone_cache.py` "Phase A reads resistance ... Phase B will read support" while the divert reads both sides, and "whose age exceeds (ttl − margin)" while the test is `>=`; in the warmer "Re-warms cached symbols before TTL expiry" and "Returns True if zones were cached" (P02-S143 to P02-S147). In the V1 observer, the flag table (`flags.py` L11 to L17), "a still-open trade is left NULL so a later run picks it up", "a manual/ad-hoc run on a non-trading day is never blocked" and "Only TERMINAL trades are finalised" beside the `NO_TRADE` writes (P03-S196; P03-S080). In the V2 path, the strong-close wording ("in the UPPER frac", "in the strong 60%"), long-only texts that remain after the short mirror of `02357130fe09fab38833e81ce59f1b0f407488bc`, "deletes the row atomically on release (confirm / reject / EOD)" while the end-of-day clear writes no status, and `sizing_inputs` "to re-size at confirm" and `timeout_at` "deadline", which are never read (P04-S163 to P04-S166). In the scripts, the spot check's undocumented 0.15 threshold and "note the result in SYSTEM_MAP"; the export's "READ-ONLY", "it never places, sizes, or persists" beside its CSV write, "a detector per as-of date" and "Run on the VM with a live token (like sr_detector_backfill)" (P05-S114, P05-S115).
- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION (texts that match the code): "3 historical fetches" and "A single serialized background worker" (P03-S195); the "non-gating" texts describe the call and the detector's own code, and say nothing of the objects the worker shares with the order path (P03-S193); "NO capital is held while parked" agrees with the divert's place before sizing (P04-S165); "Never raises" for `maybe_divert` and `register` holds (P04-S167); the field comment "naive IST (divert time = retest clock start)" agrees with the divert's naive time, while no comment states which form the monitor's clock must have (P04-S168).
- HISTORICAL: the export's role is stated in A08's own comments: the swings are "flagged NOT-YET-VALIDATED until Rama's manual-marking validation passes" and "The 03.03 R:R gate will use ANCHOR-only levels for live capital until then" (`sr_detector/models.py` L169 to L174) (P05-S116; P01-S035).

**Configuration, loader and wiring comments (A02's and A01's files; cited for their stated meaning only).**

- HISTORICAL: the configuration block's comments: `enabled` "★ SNR-V1 shadow ARMED — committed true 28-Jun (durability > the old repo-default-false/safe-revert)"; the V2 header "(DORMANT — master flag off)"; the anchors "DEFAULT-OFF (validate first)" and "Flip true AFTER the sr_level_export manual-marking validation passes"; the structure exit "Single SL owner — do NOT co-enable strategy.trailing_sl_enabled" and `sl_buffer_pct` "match Phase A"; the percentages' comments and "(fits one request per interval)", "(respects the Zerodha minute cap)" (P06-S179; P01-S123; P02-S149). The loader's docstrings: "ONE default-off flag (`enabled`) gates the whole module ... When disabled (the default) the detector is not even constructed" and the anchors "OFF (default) → detector behaviour byte-identical ... the level-export tool turns it on" (P06-S180; P03-S197).
- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION: the wiring comments "Both default OFF → nothing constructed → zero pipeline change (dormant on deploy)", "All dormant when wait_for_retest_enabled=false", "both daemons stop in _shutdown" and the structure-exit comments (P06-S181; P02-S148; P04-S169). INTERPRETATION: the "default OFF" wording of the loader docstring, the wiring comment and `PATHS.md` describes the code defaults and, for `PATHS.md`, the state before 28-Jun-2026; the V2 path, the structure exit and the anchors were each committed off with a stated condition for switching on (P06-S186). The processor's comment "the two resume-path dispatch sites are production-unreachable and deliberately uncounted" (A06's file) is a statement about production at its date (P04-S170).
- HISTORICAL: the expected-manager registry `config/expected_managers.yaml` (A02's file, A15's mechanism; "Frozen 01-Aug-2026 against deployed code 297b587") lists the detector as expected-active and the zone cache, warmer, monitor and diverter as expected-absent, with line references that match none of the baseline's lines (P02-S158; P03-S098, P03-S099; P04-S125).

**The tests (A19's; stated expectations under their own conditions, never run).**

- FACT: `tests/unit/test_sr_detector_units.py` is the one test file that imports the core's functions; its expectations cover the pivots, the clustering, the bucketing and the flags, with `ScoringParams` built directly (`band_buffer_pct` 0.0, not the configured 0.1) (P01-S124; P03-S200). INTERPRETATION: no test covers a plateau with lower neighbours, an outside bar, unsorted or duplicated candles, chaining, the round-number rule (which by code reading applies to three of the test bands), the recency factor or any NaN, infinite, zero or negative value (P01-S125, P01-S140). The fetch test's fixture rows are in descending date order and nothing sorts them (P01-S126).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: the fetch, zone-cache and anchor tests state the closure's pacing, the cache, the fail-safe, the ORB and round-number cases and the prior day as `daily[-2]` of a list ending with "today", with constant clocks; no test covers the worker loop, `start` and `stop`, the drop on a full queue or the re-warm of expired entries (P02-S150 to P02-S153). The observer tests use a naive clock and naive candle times, not the aware IST clock of production, and none covers a full queue or `stop` with queued candidates (P03-S198, P03-S199); the flags tests state no expectation for a MEDIUM zone, the nearest zone or the distances (P03-S200, P03-S201); the backfill tests do not run `main` or `_cron_main` (P03-S139, P03-S140).
- FACT and INTERPRETATION: the V2 tests (`test_sr_v2_retest_confirm.py`, `test_sr_v2_monitor.py`, `test_sr_v2_divert.py`, `test_sr_v2_continue.py`, `test_sr_v2_market_path.py`) give the monitor and the diverter naive clocks and fakes; no test runs the monitor with the wiring's aware clock, starts or stops its thread, fails a release or restores two rows of one symbol (P04-S171 to P04-S175; P04-AP20). The export's test covers only the pure core; `_make_real_analyze_fn` and `main` carry `# pragma: no cover` ("Not unit-tested (needs a broker session)") (P05-S117, P05-S118); an account of passing tests in `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` is not evidence of a run (P05-S119).

**Legacy documents (HISTORICAL; index rule 11).**

- HISTORICAL: `PATHS.md` describes the V1 master flag as "(default OFF) ... `sr_detector.enabled: false`" (L417), the observer's seam as "AFTER a successful `place()` in BOTH `_process_one` + `continue_from_gate`" (two call sites; the code has three), the V2 section as "SYMMETRIC LONG+SHORT (default-OFF, schema v38)" with the divert ":818" (baseline L1042), and the spot check as "one-time split-adjust check (VM/live)" (P01-S129; P03-S203; P04-S176; P05-S039; P06-S182). `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` describes `Candle` as "the ONE candle value object", the confidence class as never flipping "in this step", the export as a working "READ-ONLY" dump, and `enabled` as staying true (P01-S128; P03-S204; P05-S088; P06-S183).
- HISTORICAL (accounts of production, not surviving evidence): a data audit of 30-Jun-2026 reports "29 rows, all outcome-backfilled" in `sr_detector_results` (P01-S139; P03-S206); an integrity audit reports "Alive and accruing: `sr_detector_results` 270 rows, 29-Jun→31-Jul", "Deployed-tree config ground truth (grepped on the VM checkout ...)" with `wait_for_retest_enabled: false` and `watchlist.enabled: true`, and the retest paths "production-unreachable (... RETEST_% statuses = 0 all-time)" (P04-S177; P06-S185); audits of 15-Jul-2026 report that the backfill "Ran clean today" (P03-S206). These stay accounts: P01-U05, P03-U01, P03-U05, P04-U01 and P06-U01 remain UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED.
- HISTORICAL: the calibration that the V1 rows were to feed ("S&R V1 calibration — DEFERRED/collecting; reopen DATA-gated (~50 fills + ~8–10 BIR-filled W/L ...) → PASS→Phase A / RECALIBRATE→zone params"), recorded as needing "Rama's manual zone-marking pass over `scripts/sr_level_export.py` output" and as open until at least 01-Aug-2026 (P03-S205; P05-S090); no record of a result was found (P03-U09; P05-U06). A security audit of 02-Jul-2026 states that "two concurrent diverts could create two rows" in `retest_state` (P04-S178). Two legacy audits name the backfill's host-local date fallback (P03-S207). A text-match survey of 05-Aug-2026 lists the `sr_detector` keys with line numbers of an earlier state (P01-S130).
- HISTORICAL: no specification named "spec C", "spec E", "spec I" or "spec K" is tracked; "spec C" stands for three different things (the spot check, the closure's pacing, the fetcher's fail-safe) (P01-S013; P05-S108; A05 HA-09).

**History (commit messages and blame; HISTORICAL).**

- HISTORICAL: `dea336ae71c353fd86331a37d0a788e02968aca9` (2026-06-27, "SNR-DETECTOR-V1: shadow support/resistance detector (default-off, schema v37)") created the V1 package, the backfill and the spot check, with "SHADOW only — no reject/veto/SL/TGT/STM/entry change; never blocks/delays/raises into placement" and "Config: ONE default-off flag system.sr_detector.enabled" (P01-S131, P01-S132; P03-S208; P05-S015). `55c93d3ca0baff8b007b6a9a6f2f0cb603fdb362` (the same day) made the breakout flags target the just-broken zone (P03-S209). `9f56b0775163c690de977ec354176b5be5abb62c` (2026-06-27, "SNR-V2 Phase A: WAIT_FOR_RETEST entry side (default-off, schema v38)") created the V2 files, the zone cache, the warmer and the zone builder, "Dormant by default ... byte-identical" (P02-S156; P03-S210; P04-S179); `02357130fe09fab38833e81ce59f1b0f407488bc` (the short mirror) made it symmetric (P04-S180).
- HISTORICAL: `f2098656d7fb0d9a4cf917c915eee2dd909b1e41` (2026-06-28) committed `sr_detector.enabled=true` "instead of a deployed-only uncommitted override", "Deliberate trade-off (Rama): DURABILITY over the prior "repo-default false / safe-revert" convention for this flag", calling the detector "shadow / non-gating / read-only"; `a47efca136d55af804fc89d4bc9225dfa6679619` (2026-06-30) registered the backfill at 15:58 because "outcomes stayed NULL until run by hand" (P03-S214; P06-S184). `536172cfadfdd28ec9e5f8c00f9d549f0cd57dc2` (2026-07-12, the V3 shared engine, "all default-OFF (byte-identical)") added `fetch_interval`, `fetch_by_token`, the per-call lookback, the anchors, the confidence-class constants and the export script (P01-S131; P02-S157; P03-S211; P05-S121). `b3a5c949260388f51ab74a592cd9d832ee91c928` (2026-07-14) made `register()` success authoritative in the diverter (P04-S181); `09ea7254cc47b5c211fb6d408d92d3e62d859b73` (2026-07-17) added the backfill's cron entry guard (P03-S213); `132e571e161c54f0ac944c1e8e2876e8db066a9d` (2026-08-01) added the detector's telemetry handle (P03-S212). The clock lines of the V2 monitor date from its first commit (P04-S182).

**Intent conflicts between sources (reported, not resolved).**

- HISTORICAL and INTERPRETATION: on the V1 switch, the loader docstring, the wiring comment and `PATHS.md` ("default OFF") against the committed `true` and `docs/SYSTEM_MAP.md` ("`enabled` stays true") (P06-S186; P06-AP03). On the export, the comments that make its manual-marking validation the condition for the anchors and the confidence-class flip, against a script that by reading cannot produce its CSV at the baseline (P05-S089 to P05-S091; P05-AP03). On the retest path's reachability, the legacy documents' "dormant" accounts against the code facts of the monitor's clock (P04-S177; P04-S070). On the V2 path's long-only texts against the symmetric code (P04-S164). On the observer's row count, the schema's "One row per PLACED candidate" against the cases that leave none (P03-AP05). The wording of the retest path's owner across earlier records is a records difference, decided for the boundary by D-41, P1 (section 14).

## 13. Inherited items

This section lists, one row per item, everything that earlier topics' approved records hand to A08 or name A08 for: the 48 inherited items, the 16 Open Issues Register entries, the 16 AUDIT records whose Owner field names A08 outside the 48, the six prose notes N1 to N6, and the records that touch A08 code without naming A08. Every ID, owner, classification, treatment and status is copied unchanged (D-41, P4; Topic Completion Register, entry A08); A08 records its own side only; nothing is re-owned, re-classified, re-treated or closed, and no open issue's history question is answered (each stays OPEN as recorded) (P07-S001, P07-S011, P07-S068). KNOWN DEFECT occurs below only inside a copied register cell or a quotation of an earlier record; it is not a label of this STUDY. A code fact recorded here does not settle a runtime fact: where a record's result is UNKNOWN / NOT ESTABLISHED, it stays so (P07-S111, P07-S141).

Evidence: `A08_PRIOR_TOPIC_HANDOFFS_2026-11-26.md` (P07), which read the project registers and the A01 to A07 records and located the A08 code of each item, assigning a primary part (P07-S105); and the tracing statements of P01 to P06 named in the last column (each primary part's inherited-items section: P02 section 9, P03 section 10, P04 section 11, P05 section 8, P06 section 9; P01's cross-references, P01-X01).

### 13.1 The records as handed over

- HISTORICAL (record; D-41, P4; Topic Completion Register, entry A08, Scope): A08 examines "all 48 items handed to it by the approved records of A01 to A07" and the 16 Open Issues Register entries that name A08, "recording A08's side only" (P07-S011). FACT: the A08 row of each record (A01 RTR section 15, L304; the A02 to A07 RTRs' section 15.2) equals D-41's list, in content and order: A01 5, A02 7, A03 9, A04 4, A05 11, A06 8, A07 4; the AUDITs' collected index rows and the register's Scope give the same lists (P07-S012). One wording difference: the A01 row reads "C38b, probably (to confirm)", while D-41 lists C38b without that qualifier (P07-S013; P07 AP-01).

### 13.3 The 16 open issues
